Frequently Asked Questions
What is the Remove Audio from Video tool?

The Remove Audio from Video tool lets you strip the audio track from your video, quickly and easily, right in your browser, with nothing to install.

How does the Remove Audio from Video work?

Upload your video and remove its audio directly in your browser. The tool processes your footage and gives you your silent video in seconds.

Is the Remove Audio from Video free to use?

Yes, the Remove Audio from Video is free to use online. Just upload your video, remove its audio, and download the result, with no software required.

Can developers use the Remove Audio from Video programmatically?

Yes. Developers can use Cloudinary’s API to remove audio from videos and automate audio removal across their workflows at scale.

What can I do with the Remove Audio from Video?

Mute background noise, remove copyrighted music before sharing, prep clips for a voiceover, or create silent loops for social.
